		—
	<span class="post-meta-categories"><a href="https://bringatrailer.com/british/" rel="category tag">British</a></span>
<span class="post-meta-comments post-meta-comments-hide">
		| <a class="post-meta-comments-link" data-realtime_id="12111068" data-realtime_prop="comments-updated.display" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1977-mg-mgb-26/#respond">No Comments</a>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1977-mg-mgb-26/">BaT Auction: 4.2L V8-Powered 1977 MG MGB 5-Speed</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12111068"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1977-mg-mgb-26/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 4.2L V8-Powered 1977 MG MGB 5-Speed" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="414" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-620x414.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-620x414.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-768x512.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-460x307.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-155x105.jpg 155w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66-940x627.jpg 940w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1977_mg_mgb_1571765111279113f387bMG_Direct-Auto_Josh-Vaughn-Photography-66.jpg 1057w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p class="p1"><span class="s1">This 1977 MGB was reportedly acquired by the selling dealer in 2019 from the second owner of 41 years. Power is from a 4.2-liter V8 mated to a five-speed manual transmission, and other modifications include an Offenhauser intake, polyurethane suspension bushings, alloy wheels, and more. The car is finished in Sandglow yellow with a black soft top over a refurbished black leather interior. This MGB is now offered by the selling dealer with service records and a clean Tennessee title.</span></p>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1972-chevrolet-monte-carlo-4/">BaT Auction: 1972 Chevrolet Monte Carlo</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12587403"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1972-chevrolet-monte-carlo-4/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 1972 Chevrolet Monte Carlo" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="413" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113-620x413.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113-620x413.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113-768x512.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113-460x307.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113-940x627.jpg 940w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1972_chevrolet_monte_carlo_157167052598a42a7ce4P1260113.jpg 1560w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>

<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1972 Chevrolet Monte Carlo was purchased new from Webber Chevrolet in Coventry, Rhode Island and was acquired by the current owner in 2010. The car reportedly underwent a mechanical refurbishment that was completed in 2013 and included suspension updates, the installation of rear disc brakes, and more as detailed below. Power comes from a rebuilt 350ci V8 equipped with a .030″ overbore, a cut crankshaft, a mild camshaft, an Edelbrock intake manifold, and a Holley carburetor, while shifting is through a three-speed automatic transmission. This Monte Carlo is now offered on behalf of the current owner with the original uninstalled engine, window sticker, Protect-O-Plate, factory books, service records, and a clean New Jersey title.</p>

<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12036714"></a>

<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1963 Triumph TR4 reportedly was acquired by the seller in 2008 from the estate of the original owner. The seller subsequently oversaw a body-off refurbishment that was completed in 2013 and involved body work, a repaint, rebuilds of the engine and driveline, and an overhaul of the brakes. The electrical system has been converted to negative ground, and other modifications include the addition of electronic ignition and an auxiliary cooling fan as well as upgrades of the audio system. The car is finished in Spa White over a black interior and is equipped with a two-piece Surrey top. Power is from a 2.1-liter inline-four mated to a four-speed manual transmission with overdrive. This TR4 is now offered with workshop manuals, spare parts, photos of the refurbishment, and a clean Illinois title in the seller’s name.</p>

<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">

<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1979 Ferrari 308 GTB was sold new to Fran Tarkenton of the Minnesota Vikings in May 1980 at F.A.F. Motorcars, Inc. in Tucker, Georgia, and was acquired by the selling dealer in 2018. The car is finished in <i>Rosso Corsa </i>over beige leather, and power is provided by a 3.0L DOHC V8 paired with a 5-speed manual transaxle. Included service records detail work completed in 2014, which consisted of a valve adjustment, rebuilds of the carburetors and shocks, replacement of the timing belt and water pump, and more as described below. Now showing just over 19k miles, this 308 GTB is offered by the selling dealer in Illinois with the factory owner’s manuals, tool roll, service records since 2013, and a clean Montana title.</p>

Power is from a 3.5-liter S38 inline-six mated to a five-speed manual gearbox, and the car is finished in black over a gray leather interior. This M6 shows 98k miles and is offered with the owner’s manual, a clean Carfax report, service records, and a clean Oregon title in the seller’s name.</p>

<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12331529"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1998-porsche-boxster-19/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 27k-Mile 1998 Porsche Boxster" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="414" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-620x414.jpeg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-620x414.jpeg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-300x200.jpeg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-768x513.jpeg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-460x307.jpeg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-155x105.jpeg 155w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1998_porsche_boxster_15702469567dff9f98764da3DF91D72-665F-4A96-9610-D01CCCEC0F6F-e1571683799739-940x627.jpeg 940w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1998 Porsche Boxster shows 27k miles and was originally delivered to Ramsey Pontiac of Urbandale, Iowa. Finished in Arctic Silver Metallic over a Boxster Red special leather interior, the car is powered by a mid-mounted 2.5L flat-six paired with a five-speed manual transaxle. Equipment includes a black convertible soft top, a speed-activated rear spoiler, 17″ Carrera-look wheels, a leather dashboard and door panels, power-adjustable seats, automatic climate control, and a Becker Porsche CD stereo. The car remained with the original owner until it was acquired by the selling dealer earlier this year. This Boxster is now offered by the selling dealer with factory literature, a window sticker, service records, a clean Carfax report, and a clean Iowa title.</p>
</div>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/2000-jaguar-xjr-9/">BaT Auction: 2000 Jaguar XJR</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=11543161"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/2000-jaguar-xjr-9/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 2000 Jaguar XJR" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="413" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/2000_jaguar_xjr_15701733208f4872373CAM_0573-e1571762893942-620x413.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/2000_jaguar_xjr_15701733208f4872373CAM_0573-e1571762893942-620x413.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/2000_jaguar_xjr_15701733208f4872373CAM_0573-e1571762893942-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/2000_jaguar_xjr_15701733208f4872373CAM_0573-e1571762893942-768x511.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/2000_jaguar_xjr_15701733208f4872373CAM_0573-e1571762893942-460x306.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/2000_jaguar_xjr_15701733208f4872373CAM_0573-e1571762893942-940x625.jpg 940w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 2000 Jaguar XJR is finished in Anthracite over Charcoal leather and shows just over 65k miles. Power is provided by a supercharged 4.0L V8 mated to a five-speed automatic transmission, and features include a power sunroof, cruise control, heated front seats, fog lamps, automatic climate control, chrome trim, and 18″ alloy wheels. Acquired by seller earlier this year, the car has since been serviced with new tires, a battery, and an oil and filter change. This XJR is now offered with factory books and manuals, a clean Carfax report, and a clean Pennsylvania title in the seller’s name.</p>
</div>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1970-porsche-914-6-25/">BaT Auction: 1970 Porsche 914-6</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12422044"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1970-porsche-914-6-25/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 1970 Porsche 914-6" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="413" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1970_porsche_914-6_157063946598764da1970-Porsche-914-6-100-620x413.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1970_porsche_914-6_157063946598764da1970-Porsche-914-6-100-620x413.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1970_porsche_914-6_157063946598764da1970-Porsche-914-6-100-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1970_porsche_914-6_157063946598764da1970-Porsche-914-6-100-768x512.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1970_porsche_914-6_157063946598764da1970-Porsche-914-6-100-460x307.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1970_porsche_914-6_157063946598764da1970-Porsche-914-6-100-940x627.jpg 940w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1970 Porsche 914-6 spent time in California and Nevada before being imported to British Columbia in 2010 and acquired by the selling dealer last year. Finished in Light Ivory over a black leatherette interior, the car is powered by a numbers-matching 2.0L flat-six paired with a five-speed manual transaxle. A refurbishment was reportedly initiated by a longterm previous owner and completed by Rudi &amp; Company of Victoria, British Columbia under subsequent ownership. Work included a repaint, re-chroming the bumpers, installing new window glass, and replacing the radio. Approximately 1,000 miles are said to have been added following the refresh, and a new clutch has since been installed along with a new crankshaft seal, fuel pump, and spark plugs. This 914-6 is now being offered with service records beginning in 1985, a Porsche Certificate of Authenticity, and Ontario registration.</p>
</div>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1931-ford-model-a-29/">BaT Auction: 1931 Ford Model A Sport Coupe</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12298745"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1931-ford-model-a-29/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 1931 Ford Model A Sport Coupe" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="413" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1931_ford_model_a_1571769322391b1DSC_2465-620x413.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1931_ford_model_a_1571769322391b1DSC_2465-620x413.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1931_ford_model_a_1571769322391b1DSC_2465-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1931_ford_model_a_1571769322391b1DSC_2465-768x512.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1931_ford_model_a_1571769322391b1DSC_2465-460x307.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1931_ford_model_a_1571769322391b1DSC_2465-940x627.jpg 940w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1931 Ford Model A Sport Coupe was acquired by its current owner and her husband 27 years ago, and was subsequently repainted in brown and black. The car features a rumble seat and a fixed fabric roof over a tan cloth interior, and power is from a 201ci inline-four paired with a three-speed manual transmission. Modifications include hydraulic brakes as well as a 12-volt electrical system with an alternator. The carburetor was serviced and the coolant and fuel were changed in preparation for the sale. This Model A is registered on California non-operative status and offered by the seller on behalf of its late owner’s widow with a custom cover and a clean California title.</p>
</div>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1989-honda-gb500-4/">BaT Auction: 1989 Honda GB500 Tourist Trophy</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12139285"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1989-honda-gb500-4/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 1989 Honda GB500 Tourist Trophy" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="413" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1989_honda_gb500_1571323307540a6d8330399e24dMG_5103-620x413.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1989_honda_gb500_1571323307540a6d8330399e24dMG_5103-620x413.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1989_honda_gb500_1571323307540a6d8330399e24dMG_5103-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1989_honda_gb500_1571323307540a6d8330399e24dMG_5103-768x512.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1989_honda_gb500_1571323307540a6d8330399e24dMG_5103-460x307.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/1989_honda_gb500_1571323307540a6d8330399e24dMG_5103-940x627.jpg 940w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1989 Honda GB500 Tourist Trophy was purchased by the selling dealer three years ago, reportedly from its third owner. Power is provided by a single overhead-cam 498cc single paired with a five-speed transmission, and the bike is finished in black paint with gold striping. This GB500 TT is offered by the selling dealer in California with an Ohio title.</p>

<h2 class="post-title"><a class="post-title-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1976-ford-bronco-11/">BaT Auction: 1976 Ford Bronco</a></h2>
<div class="post-image-link-container excerpt-post-image-link-container">
<a class="auctions-item-watch" href="https://bringatrailer.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=bat_listing_watch&amp;listing=12289359"></a>
<a class="post-image-link" href="https://bringatrailer.com/listing/1976-ford-bronco-11/">
<img alt="BaT Auction: 1976 Ford Bronco" class="post-image wp-post-image" height="413" sizes="(max-width: 620px) 100vw, 620px" src="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1976_ford_bronco_1570248300ee9b8451DSC01806-e1571692498669-620x413.jpg" srcset="https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1976_ford_bronco_1570248300ee9b8451DSC01806-e1571692498669-620x413.jpg 620w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1976_ford_bronco_1570248300ee9b8451DSC01806-e1571692498669-300x200.jpg 300w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1976_ford_bronco_1570248300ee9b8451DSC01806-e1571692498669-768x512.jpg 768w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1976_ford_bronco_1570248300ee9b8451DSC01806-e1571692498669-460x307.jpg 460w, https://cdn.bringatrailer.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/1976_ford_bronco_1570248300ee9b8451DSC01806-e1571692498669-940x627.jpg 940w" width="620"/> <span class="auctions-item-image-overlay"></span> </a>
</div>
<div class="post-excerpt">
<p>This 1976 Ford Bronco was reportedly refurbished by a previous owner while in Oregon between 2007 and 2008, including cosmetic and mechanical upgrades. It is powered by a 302ci V8 paired with a C4 three-speed automatic transmission and Dana 20 two-speed transfer case. The truck was imported to Canada in 2009, and the seller purchased it approximately four months ago. Highlights include a lifted and modified suspension, Dick Cepek wheels with 33″ tires, leather bucket seats, and more as described below. This Bronco is now offered in Britsh Columbia with a steel half-cab hardtop, a full-coverage soft top, a bikini top, partial records back to 2006, and clean British Columbia registration in the seller’s name.</p>
</div>
